These findings suggest that the educational value of AI-generated feedback depends not only on the quality of feedback comments, but also on workflows that actively structure students' enactment of feedback literacy processes. The results have implications for the design of AI feedback systems that position learners as active participants in judgement, dialogue, and improvement rather than passive recipients of comments. Overall findings show that AI access alone is insufficient; purposeful workflow design is central to productive feedback use.

</details>


### [232] [CLAIM: Leading Open-domain Active Clarification of Large Language Models with Uncertainty Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11631)
*Kuangzhao Yang,Ziliang Zhao,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CLAIM框架，一个基于不确定性驱动的主动澄清学习框架，通过熵驱动的合成数据生成管线，消除对人工标注和偏好对齐的依赖，训练统一的澄清决策模型（SFT+GRPO），在开放域人机交互中实现稳定可泛化的澄清策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放域人机交互中，LLM面临模糊或不完整的用户查询，直接回答往往导致过度泛化、错误或低信息量的响应。虽然澄清问题能显著提升交互质量，但现有方法严重依赖人工标注数据或偏好对齐来处理两个核心挑战（何时需要澄清、澄清查询的哪个方面），导致标注成本高且泛化受限。

Method: CLAIM通过多模型回答不一致产生的熵来量化查询不确定性，替代显式人工偏好标注。核心管线包括：(1) 熵驱动的合成数据生成管线，整合基于熵的不确定性估计、语义聚类和基于推理的判断，实现澄清需求的可靠自动标注；(2) 将澄清过程形式化为结构化决策生成问题，采用SFT与组相对策略优化（GRPO）相结合的联合训练范式，训练统一的澄清决策模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，CLAIM在无需人工标注数据的情况下能够学习到稳定且可泛化的澄清策略，为开放域LLM交互中的主动理解提供了低成本、鲁棒的解决方案。

Conclusion: CLAIM验证了不确定性驱动方法在主动澄清学习中的有效性，通过熵信号构建高质量合成数据并结合SFT+GRPO训练范式，成功替代了昂贵的显式人工标注和偏好对齐流程，为开放域人机交互中的主动理解提供了一种低成本、可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: In open-domain human-computer interaction scenarios, large language models (LLMs) frequently encounter user queries that are ambiguous or incomplete. In such cases, directly producing an answer often leads to overgeneralized, erroneous, or low-information responses. In contrast, asking clarifying questions can substantially improve interaction quality. However, existing approaches still rely heavily on manually annotated data or preference alignment to address two fundamental challenges: when clarification is necessary, and which aspect of the query should be clarified. This reliance incurs high annotation costs and limits generalization. To address these challenges, we propose CLAIM, an uncertainty-driven framework for active clarification learning in open-domain settings. CLAIM eliminates the need for explicit human preference annotations by quantifying query uncertainty through the entropy induced by answer disagreements across multiple models. This uncertainty signal is then used to construct high-quality synthetic data, enabling the training of a unified clarification decision model through a combination of supervised learning and reinforcement learning. Specifically, we propose an entropy-driven synthetic data generation pipeline that integrates entropy-based uncertainty estimation with semantic clustering and reasoning-based judgments, enabling reliable automatic annotation of clarification requirements. To train CLAIM, we formulate the clarification process as a structured decision generation problem and adopt a training paradigm that combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with group-relative policy optimization (GRPO). Experimental results demonstrate that CLAIM can learn stable and generalizable clarification strategies without relying on manually labeled data, offering a low-cost and robust solution for proactive understanding in real-world open-domain interactions with LLMs.

</details>


### [233] [XBridge: Entity-Grounded Latent Bridge for Heterogeneous LLM Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11676)
*Wooseong Yang,Wei-Chieh Huang,Weizhi Zhang,Yu Wang,Philip S. Yu,Junhyun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出XBRIDGE，一种跨架构异构多智能体LLM系统的无解码通信协议，通过词汇锚点映射（LAM）和潜在富集桥（LEB）解决跨架构通信中的实体接地问题（罕见词压缩坍缩），在七个基准上全面超越文本通信并显著降低延迟，且仅需少量可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多智能体LLM系统（由不同模型家族驱动的智能体）通过减少冗余推理模式可以超越同构配置，但现有通信协议要么仅通过文本传递信息（丢失发送方内部表示），要么需要架构同质性才能进行潜在级别传输。作者识别出跨架构通信中的"实体接地问题"：跨注意力桥在不同LLM家族间传输连续表示时遭遇罕见词压缩坍缩，导致实体身份在连续瓶颈中丢失（桥接F1仅约30%）。

Method: XBRIDGE包含两个核心机制：(1) 词汇锚点映射（LAM）将发送方的原始上下文token映射到接收方的词汇表，提供离散实体锚点；(2) 潜在富集桥（LEB）允许接收方查询发送方的隐藏状态以获取上下文富集。实体锚点通过接收方自身的自注意力机制将桥接的上下文信号锚定到特定实体上，从而解决实体接地问题。

Result: 在三个模型家族（Llama、Qwen和Mistral）、七个基准以及两个通信方向上，XBRIDGE在所有七个任务的每对模型组合中均优于基于文本的通信，并实现11倍更低的延迟；在相同架构设置下，它也在七个任务中的六个上超越了KV共享基线。LEB仅需2.64亿个可训练参数（接收方的3.8%），在小规模平衡样本集上训练，且推理开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: XBRIDGE通过结合离散实体锚点与连续潜在富集，有效解决了异构LLM间通信的实体接地瓶颈，在准确性、延迟和参数效率上同时实现了显著优势，为跨架构多智能体系统的高效通信提供了新范式。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multi-agent LLM systems, where agents are powered by different model families, can outperform homogeneous configurations by reducing redundant reasoning patterns. Yet existing communication protocols either operate through text, discarding the sender's internal representations, or require architectural homogeneity for latent-level transfer. We identify the entity grounding problem in cross-architecture communication: cross-attention bridges that transfer continuous representations across different LLM families suffer from rare-token compression collapse, where entity identity is lost in the continuous bottleneck (bridge-only F1 ~30%). We propose XBRIDGE, a decode-free communication protocol that addresses this through two mechanisms. Lexical Anchor Mapping (LAM) maps the sender's original context tokens to the receiver's vocabulary, providing discrete entity anchors. A Latent Enrichment Bridge (LEB) lets the receiver query the sender's hidden states for contextual enrichment. The entity anchors ground the bridge's contextual signals to specific entities through the receiver's own self-attention. Across three model families (Llama, Qwen, and Mistral), seven benchmarks, and both communication directions, XBRIDGE outperforms text-based communication on all seven tasks for each model pair while achieving 11x lower latency, and in a same-architecture setting it also exceeds a KV-sharing baseline on six of seven tasks. LEB requires only 264M trainable parameters (3.8% of the receiver), is trained on a small balanced sample set, and adds negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [234] [AgenticTwin: An Agentic LLM Framework Integrated with Digital Twin for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11679)
*Touseef Hasan,Mounika Ghanta,Souvika Sarkar,Ujjwal Guin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticTwin，一个将大语言模型推理与数字孪生异常检测管道相结合的多智能体框架，并引入基于真实天气传感器数据与合成异常的基准评估流程，验证轻量级开源LLM在异常诊断、上下文检索和缓解建议方面的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生管道中检测到的异常即使由经验丰富的操作员解释也极具挑战性，因为原始传感器数据的复杂性和体量使得深入分析困难。尽管LLM在推理和解释方面展现出强大能力，但其在数字孪生驱动的异常分析中的集成仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出AgenticTwin智能体框架，将LLM驱动的推理与基于数字孪生的异常检测流水线集成。框架将LLM生成的解释锚定在数字孪生异常分类器的输出上，并允许操作员以自然语言查询系统。同时构建了一个基于真实天气传感器数据注入合成异常的基准评估管道，用于生成受控的操作员查询，并评估部署轻量级开源LLM的可行性。

Result: 实验结果表明，结构化智能体协作与知识锚定推理在多种异常场景下显著提升了诊断质量、上下文检索效果和缓解建议质量。

Conclusion: AgenticTwin框架证明了将LLM以智能体化方式集成到数字孪生异常分析流程中的有效性，结构化智能体协作与知识锚定推理是提升异常诊断质量的关键因素，且轻量级开源LLM在实际信息物理环境中具备部署可行性。

Abstract: Digital twins are increasingly used to monitor and simulate the behavior of cyber-physical systems. Even with skilled operators, interpreting anomalies detected within digital twin pipelines is challenging, as the sheer complexity and volume of raw sensor data make thorough analysis difficult. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and explanation, yet their integration into digital twin-driven anomaly analysis remains underexplored. In this work, we propose AgenticTwin, an agentic framework that integrates LLM-driven reasoning with a digital twin-based anomaly detection pipeline. The framework grounds LLM-generated explanations in outputs from a digital twin-driven anomaly classifier and enables human operators to ask relevant natural-language questions about the system. Beyond the framework itself, we introduce a benchmark-oriented evaluation pipeline constructed over synthetic anomalies injected into a real-world weather sensor dataset, enabling controlled generation of operator queries over anomaly events. We further evaluate the feasibility of deploying lightweight, open-source LLMs for practical cyber-physical environments. Experimental results demonstrate that structured agent collaboration and knowledge-grounded reasoning improve diagnosis quality, contextual retrieval, and mitigation quality across diverse possible anomaly scenarios.

</details>


### [235] [FrontierFinance: A Challenging Benchmark for Measuring Frontier Intelligence of Finance Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11683)
*Yuhao Zhang,O. Ozan Koyluoglu,Thejas Venkatesh,Richard Diehl Martinez,Vishank Bhatia,Arash Alidoust,Ashwin Paranjape*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FrontierFinance是一个全新的开源金融研究基准，包含220个专家级查询和11,543条带来源的评分准则，覆盖投资者工作流的六大关键用例。评估发现工具框架比模型本身更影响质量和效率，Samaya内部系统以56.0%领先最强的专有模型（49.2%），最佳开源模型（46.4%）以4.5倍更低成本接近最佳专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准仅聚焦数据提取这一狭窄任务，模型已趋于饱和；同时基于参考的指标和通用LLM评判在开放式长回答上失效。缺少能捕捉完整投资者工作流复杂性的基准。

Method: 构建包含220个专家编写查询和11,543条带来源归因的评分准则的开源基准，覆盖投资者工作流6大用例；在统一框架下评估前沿模型和代理系统；数据集和评分代码公开。

Result: 工具框架而非模型单独决定质量和效率；Samaya内部系统以56.0%领先（Claude Fable 5为49.2%，成本约2.2倍更低）；最佳开源模型Kimi K3（46.4%）以4.5倍更低成本接近最佳专有模型；筛选与发现（33%）和行业/宏观（39%）两个用例是最难的任务。

Conclusion: FrontierFinance是比现有公开金融基准更广更难的评估体系，揭示了完整投资者工作流中模型与工具框架的协同重要性，并指出当前系统在筛选发现和宏观行业分析上仍存在显著瓶颈。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed for professional investment research, yet no benchmark captures the complexity of the full investor workflow. Existing benchmarks mainly target financial data extraction, a narrow slice that current models have largely saturated, while reference-based metrics and generic LLM-as-a-judge scoring fall short on the open-ended, long-form answers that real analyst queries demand. We introduce FrontierFinance, a fully open benchmark of 220 expert-crafted queries and 11,543 source-attributed rubrics spanning six crucial use cases across the full investor workflow. FrontierFinance is both broader and harder than existing public finance benchmarks. Evaluating frontier models and agent systems under a common harness restricted to publicly available data, we find that the tool harness, not the model alone, strongly shapes quality and efficiency; that Samaya's in-house system leads at 56.0%, ahead of the strongest frontier model (Claude Fable 5, 49.2%) at roughly 2.2x lower cost; and that the best open-weight model (Kimi K3, 46.4%) nearly matches the best proprietary model at 4.5x lower cost. Screening & Discovery and Sector, Industry & Macro remain the hardest use cases across all systems, where even the best systems reach only 33% and 39%. We make the dataset and grading code publicly available.

</details>


### [236] [HUGIN: Enhancing Vision-Language Planning for Autonomous Logistics Sorting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11692)
*Xikai Sun,Cangtian Zhou,Kebin Liu,Ke Ma,Xu Wang,Zaishu Chen,Haotian Wang,Li Liu,Yunhao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HUGIN训练框架，解决自主物流分拣系统中跨多摄像头视角的联合多场景理解（JMSU）问题，通过内源数据增强和全局上下文排序两大组件，使视觉语言模型在长视觉上下文中实现跨场景的开放世界理解与任务规划，在SortingBench基准上将Qwen3-VL-8B准确率从63.6%提升至78.8%，并在超15,000件包裹的实际部署中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主物流分拣系统（ALSS）是具身智能的重要工业应用，需要跨空间不连续相机视角的联合规划。现有的视觉语言模型（VLM）直接应用于该场景面临两大挑战：跨场景标注稀缺以及长视觉上下文导致的注意力分散问题。因此需要专门的方法来适配VLM到JMSU任务。

Method: 提出HUGIN训练框架，包含两个互补组件：1) 内源数据增强（Endogenous Data Augmentation），在操作约束下重组已验证的原子事实以生成更多训练数据；2) 全局上下文排序（Global Context Ranking），通过对比学习使指令表示与完整视觉上下文的关联强于与部分视觉上下文的关联。同时构建了SortingBench数据集和基准，涵盖四种ALSS布局。

Result: 在五种开源VLM上，HUGIN一致优于匹配基线；Qwen3-VL-8B在SortingBench上的准确率从63.6%提升到78.8%。消融实验验证了各组件有效性，且在超过15,000件包裹的部署测试中证实了VLM规划在自主物流分拣中的实际可行性。

Conclusion: HUGIN有效解决了VLM在JMSU任务中跨场景监督稀缺和注意力分散的问题，显著提升了多视角联合场景理解的性能，并展示了该方法在具身任务中的溢出收益及工业部署的实用性。

Abstract: Autonomous logistics sorting systems (ALSS) are an important industrial application of embodied AI, which requires joint planning over spatially disjoint camera views. We formulate this setting as Joint Multi-Scene Understanding (JMSU). With open-world visual understanding and task-planning capabilities, vision-language models (VLMs) are promising candidates for JMSU. However, directly applying existing VLMs to JMSU is non-trivial due to scarce cross-scene supervision and attention dispersion caused by long visual context in JMSU. To address these challenges, we propose HUGIN, a training framework with two complementary components. Endogenous Data Augmentation recombines verified atomic facts under operating constraints, while Global Context Ranking aligns the instruction representation more strongly with the complete visual context than with a partial visual context. To support ongoing research, we construct a high-quality industrial sorting dataset and benchmark named SortingBench from four layouts of autonomous logistics sorting systems. Across five open VLMs, HUGIN consistently outperforms matched baselines; for example, the accuracy on SortingBench of Qwen3-VL-8B increases from 63.6% to 78.8%. Additional experiments verify the effectiveness of each component and JMSU's spillover benefits in embodied tasks. Deployment tests involving more than 15,000 packages support the practical viability of VLM-based planning for autonomous logistics sorting.

</details>


### [237] [Reproducing and Stress-Testing Two Approaches to LLM Reasoning Reliability: Test-Time Probability Aggregation and Logic-Representation Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08514)
*Minhan Cho,Jimin Kweon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文独立复现了两种提升LLM推理可靠性的方法（RPC和LCF），并跨领域、跨模型进行压力测试。结果显示：RPC在作者发布的推理路径上能精确复现原始结果，但在四个新领域上对自我一致性（self-consistency）的提升均不显著；LCF的"逻辑有效性"方向虽然存在但较弱，且其正面效果不显著，在部分模型上反而显著降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这两种方法（RPC和LCF）的原始评估均由各自作者自己进行，从未被独立复现或跨模型、跨领域压力测试过；此外LCF未公开代码，因此其可靠性声明值得独立验证。

Method: 作者独立重跑了RPC的已发布路径聚合方法，并从头重新实现了LCF的投影器（projector）、对比学习（contrastive）和干预（intervention）流水线。然后将两种方法扩展到四个新任务领域：文本到SQL、法律文本抽取、谬误识别、先例评分，并对LCF的表征进行了直接探测。

Result: RPC在作者发布的推理路径上精确复现了原始网格结果；但在四个新领域上，其对自我一致性的优势从无显著（配对p≥0.28）。在BIRD数据集上预算变化时，RPC的优势随K增长但与预测一致，但最大差距（+2.5精度，K=32，p=0.16）在样本扩大至n=200时反转（-0.25）。LCF的逻辑有效性方向真实但较弱（最佳子层可分离性0.82 vs 语义属性对照的0.95）；它在Qwen3上的唯一正面效果不显著（p=0.56），而在另外三个模型中有两个显著降低了ΔProb。

Conclusion: 两种方法在独立压力测试下均未表现出稳定的显著优势：RPC的优势在小样本或单一领域中可能存在但缺乏统计稳健性；LCF的干预效果微弱且在部分模型上有负面影响。这些发现强调了在LLM可靠性方法上独立复现和跨设置验证的重要性。

Abstract: We independently reproduce two recent methods for making large language model (LLM) reasoning more reliable, and stress-test them across domains and models (RPC across four new task domains with Qwen3-8B, LCF across four 7-8B models). The first, RPC, aggregates token probabilities and self-consistency at inference; the second, LCF, trains projectors that split hidden states into "content" and "logic" and edits the logic part toward a valid region. Validating such reliability claims matters because the original evaluations are run by each method's own authors and were never independently reproduced or stress-tested across models and domains, and LCF shipped no public code. We re-run RPC's published-path aggregation and re-implement LCF's projector, contrastive, and intervention pipeline, then extend both to text-to-SQL, legal extraction, fallacy identification, and precedent grading, and probe LCF's representation directly. RPC reproduces the original grid exactly on the authors' released reasoning paths; on four new domains its edge over self-consistency is never significant (ties or small mixed differences, paired p >= 0.28), and on BIRD, the one domain where we vary the budget, the edge grows with K as predicted but its largest gap (+2.5 accuracy at K=32, p=0.16) reverses to -0.25 when we enlarge the sample to n=200. LCF's logic-validity direction is real but weak (0.82 separability at the single best sub-layer versus 0.95 for a semantic-attribute control); its one positive effect (Qwen3 $Δ$Prob) is not significant (p=0.56), while it significantly reduces $Δ$Prob on two of the other three models.

</details>


### [238] [Making Your LLMs More Objective: Stabilizing LLM Safety Behavior Across Traits with Trait-Invariant Safety Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11705)
*Lang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型的安全行为会受到系统提示中不同性格特质设定的影响，同一请求在不同特质下可能得到不同的安全决策。作者提出了基于拒答的评估指标（Trait-Induced Deviation和Trait-Induced Flip Rate），并从表示层面揭示了特质是在低维子空间内扰动模型的安全表征。基于此提出了Trait-Invariant Safety Tuning (TIST)框架及其实例化方法Trait-Subspace Neutralization (TraSN)，通过在特质子空间内强制不变性来提升跨特质的安全稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐的大语言模型理应根据用户请求的内容表现安全行为（拒绝不安全请求、遵从安全请求），但研究发现相同请求在不同系统提示设定的性格特质下会产生截然不同的安全决策，这种特质诱导的安全变异性是一个未被充分认识的安全漏洞，需要系统的度量和缓解方法。

Method: 提出两个基于拒答的评估指标：Trait-Induced Deviation（衡量数据集层面偏离无特质基线的程度）和Trait-Induced Flip Rate（衡量同一请求在不同特质间是否获得不同安全决策）。表示层面分析发现特质在低维子空间内扰动安全表征。提出Trait-Invariant Safety Tuning (TIST)自蒸馏框架，将特质条件下的行为与无特质基线行为对齐；并基于子空间分析提出Trait-Subspace Neutralization (TraSN)，仅在识别出的特质子空间内强制执行不变性。

Result: 实验表明TraSN在提升特质不变安全性和增强有害请求安全性的同时保持了通用能力。识别出特质是影响LLM安全和鲁棒行为的重要因素。

Conclusion: 特质是LLM安全行为中一个此前被忽视的重要影响因素。通过在特质子空间中执行不变性约束，可以有效缓解特质诱导的安全变异性，实现跨特质稳定的安全行为，同时不牺牲模型的一般能力。

Abstract: Aligned large language models (LLMs) are expected to exhibit safety behavior based on the content of the user request: they should refuse unsafe requests and comply with safe ones. However, we show that the same request can elicit substantially different safety decisions under different traits assigned in the system prompt, a failure mode we call trait-induced safety variation. To measure this failure, we introduce refusal-based metrics: Trait-Induced Deviation measures dataset-level deviation from the no-trait baseline, while Trait-Induced Flip Rate measures whether the same request receives different safety decisions across traits. We then provide a representation-level analysis of the mechanism behind trait-induced safety shifts and find that traits perturb the model's safety representations within a low-dimensional subspace. To achieve trait-invariant safety, where safety behavior remains stable across traits, we introduce Trait-Invariant Safety Tuning (TIST), a simple yet effective self-distillation framework that aligns an LLM's trait-conditioned behavior with its no-trait behavior. Guided by our analysis, we further propose Trait-Subspace Neutralization (TraSN), an instantiation of TIST, which enforces invariance only within the identified trait subspace. Experiments show that TraSN improves trait-invariant safety and strengthens harmful-request safety while preserving general capability. Our results highlight traits as an important factor in LLM safety and robust model behavior.

</details>


### [239] [Proportional Analogies on Probability Distributions via Bayesian Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11724)
*Pierre-Alexandre Murena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将比例类比（proportional analogy）的框架扩展到概率分布领域，基于贝叶斯更新提出了一种新的类比形式化方法，并在指数族分布及高斯混合近似上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比例类比已在布尔域、符号域和实值域上得到广泛研究，但其在概率分布上的扩展仍然空白。作者旨在填补这一空白，将类比推理的严格公理化框架引入概率分布这一更丰富的数据表示空间。

Method: 基于贝叶斯更新定义概率分布之间的比例类比关系：两个分布相关联当且仅当其中一个可以通过适当的观测集合诱导的贝叶斯更新变换为另一个。文中研究了该框架在指数族多个标准成员上的具体表现，并通过高斯混合近似将其推广到任意概率分布。

Result: 成功在概率分布上建立了比例类比的形式化框架，验证了其在指数族分布（如高斯族等）上的适用性，并展示了通过高斯混合近似可将该方法自然扩展到任意概率分布。

Conclusion: 该工作为概率分布上的类比推理提供了首个公理化框架，贝叶斯更新作为核心机制使得类比关系具有概率语义解释，高斯混合近似则保证了方法的普适性，为未来在概率机器学习中应用类比推理铺平了道路。

Abstract: Analogies are quaternary relations of the form "A is to B as C is to D". Among the various formalizations of analogical reasoning, proportional analogies provide an important axiomatic framework by characterizing valid analogies through a set of postulates. While proportional analogies have been extensively studied over Boolean, symbolic, and real-valued domains, their extension to probability distributions remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we introduce a notion of proportional analogy for probability distributions based on Bayesian updating. Our approach builds upon the idea that two distributions are related whenever one can be transformed into the other through Bayesian updating induced by a suitable set of observations. We investigate this framework for several standard members of the exponential family and discuss how it naturally extends to arbitrary probability distributions through Gaussian mixture approximations.

</details>


### [240] [Harness-IF: Evaluating Instruction Following Across Instruction Surfaces in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11727)
*Zining Huang,Haoran Que,Hong Zeng,Ge Zhang,Zuo Wang,Jin Chen,Haodong Wang,Zhongfei Hou,Changxin Pu,Shen Yan,Wenhao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harness-IF基准，用于从执行证据中逐条评分编码智能体的操作规则遵守情况，并引入Against-Prior Accuracy（AP-Acc）指标以区分真实遵从与偶然巧合。在12个前沿模型上，AP-Acc显著低于总体准确率，表明聚合分数高估了遵从性，且发现规则优先级不随提示层级深浅排列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令遵循基准将规则集中在用户轮次中，而编码智能体基准过于强调最终任务成功，两者都无法判断智能体遵守规则是出于真实遵从还是碰巧本来就打算这么做。缺乏能区分"遵守"与"巧合"的评估方法。

Method: 构建包含642条规则的规则库，从中抽取60个真实多轮编码任务，256条规则获得判定结果，并将规则放置于部署智能体可读取的五个可配置表面。提出AP-Acc指标：仅对标记为"违背无提示默认行为"的规则计分，通过九种探针构建中撤除规则重新运行任务进行观测。另设对抗性平衡冲突实验，考察九种独立构建上的规则优先级。

Result: 在12个前沿模型中，整体准确率范围72.1%-85.9%，AP-Acc范围66.1%-78.6%。每个模型在against-prior规则上表现更差，差距为3.6至7.4个百分点（均值5.81），该趋势在常见支持分析与条目聚类区间分析中均成立。聚合分数以模型特异的幅度高估遵从性：先验控制不改变顶尖模型排名，但交换了三组相邻排名对。冲突实验表明，系统提示、项目文件和用户指令优先于工具描述和技能描述，优先级并不随提示深度排列。

Conclusion: Harness-IF通过执行证据逐条评估规则遵从，AP-Acc能有效分离真实遵从与巧合行为。现有聚合指标系统性高估编码智能体的规则遵从能力，且不同模型的夸大程度不同。规则优先级不由提示深度决定，这对提示设计具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: When a coding agent obeys a rule, it may simply have been going to do that anyway. Existing instruction-following benchmarks cannot tell the difference: they concentrate rules in the user turn, while coding-agent benchmarks emphasize final task success. We introduce Harness-IF, which scores operational rules one at a time from execution evidence: 60 realistic multi-turn coding items drawn from a 642-rule library, 256 rules receiving verdicts, placed on the five configurable surfaces a deployed agent reads. To separate compliance from coincidence we introduce Against-Prior Accuracy (AP-Acc), which scores only rules labeled as opposing unprompted defaults, observed by re-running tasks with the rule withheld across nine probe builds and curated otherwise. Across 12 frontier models, accuracy spans 72.1-85.9% and AP-Acc 66.1-78.6%; every model is worse on against-prior rules, by 3.6 to 7.4 points (mean 5.81), and the direction survives a common-support analysis with item-clustered intervals. Aggregate scores therefore overstate compliance by a model-specific margin: prior control leaves the top build unchanged and exchanges three adjacent rank pairs. A counterbalanced conflict pilot on nine separate builds adds a second result: pooled precedence does not follow prompt depth, with system prompts, project files, and user instructions ahead of tool and skill descriptions.

</details>


### [241] [HyperANFIS: Enhancing Rule Representation and Interpretability in Adaptive Neuro-Fuzzy Systems via Hyperbolic Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11768)
*Haoran Pei,Zhao Su,Zetao Lin,Haoran Li,Jun Shen,Qi Zhu,Lan Guo,Qingguo Zhou,Binbin Yong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperANFIS，一种将自适应神经模糊推理系统（ANFIS）扩展到双曲空间的变体。HyperANFIS保留传统ANFIS的模糊语义和核心架构，但将规则原型学习、规则激活和结论聚合转移到双曲空间中进行，利用双曲几何的表示特性增强模糊推理过程，在预测精度、规则间协作和可解释规则的可信度方面均优于标准ANFIS及其变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ANFIS模型通常在欧几里得空间中构建规则前件和执行推理，其表示能力和预测性能受到限制。作者希望通过利用双曲几何的表示特性来解决这一局限性，从而提升模糊推理系统的性能。

Method: 提出Hyperbolic ANFIS（HyperANFIS），将传统ANFIS中的规则原型学习、规则激活和结论聚合操作迁移到双曲空间中进行，同时保留ANFIS的模糊语义和核心架构，使其仍能生成可解释的IF-THEN模糊规则。

Result: 实验表明HyperANFIS在所有数据集上一致优于标准ANFIS基线及多种ANFIS变体，并且生成的模糊规则质量更高。

Conclusion: 双曲空间中的模糊推理能够有效增强ANFIS的预测能力、规则间协作效率以及可解释规则的可靠性，为神经模糊推理系统提供了一个有前景的改进方向。

Abstract: The adaptive neuro-fuzzy inference system (ANFIS) is an interpretable reasoning framework capable of generating explicit IF-THEN fuzzy rules, making it suitable for tasks requiring transparent reasoning. However, existing ANFIS models generally construct rule antecedents and perform inference in Euclidean space, limiting their representational capacity and predictive performance. To address this issue, we propose Hyperbolic ANFIS (HyperANFIS), a hyperbolic extension of ANFIS. HyperANFIS preserves the fuzzy semantics and core architecture of conventional ANFIS while performing rule-prototype learning, rule activation, and consequent aggregation in hyperbolic space. It also retains the ability to generate interpretable IF-THEN rules. By exploiting the representational properties of hyperbolic geometry, HyperANFIS strengthens the fuzzy inference process, thereby improving predictive accuracy, inter-rule collaboration, and the credibility of its interpretable rules. Experimental results show that HyperANFIS consistently outperforms the standard ANFIS baseline and various ANFIS variants across all datasets, while also generating higher-quality fuzzy rules.

</details>


### [242] [The Sleeping Agent: What Gist-Based Context Compression Loses and Why](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11775)
*Nicholas E. Kyrkewood*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用受睡眠记忆巩固启发的压缩框架SWC（显著性加权巩固）作为诊断探针，研究gist压缩对不同类型记忆检索的影响。在LoCoMo的全部10个对话上以温度0评估四种条件，发现gist压缩在多跳推理和单跳事实问题上优于截断，但在时间类问题上显著失败，原因是gist抽象提示丢弃了日期和时间。通过一行提示修改可将时间表达保留率从3.05%提升至62.39%，并使时间类问题的judge准确率恢复+0.314。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程语言模型代理中，基于gist的上下文压缩是常见方法，但压缩对不同类型记忆检索的影响尚不明确。作者希望系统性理解gist压缩何时有帮助、何时会带来损害，并定位具体失败机制。

Method: 提出SWC框架作为诊断探针：按显著性对对话历史打分、分为优先级层级、对中优先级内容进行结构化gist抽象。在LoCoMo全部10个对话上评估四种条件（含全上下文、截断、压缩等），共1,935个匹配的纯文本问题（排除对抗性的第5类后主聚合用1,501个），温度设为0。并引入保留率分析（temporal expression preservation）以追踪压缩过程中的信息丢失机制，最后通过修改gist提示来验证修复效果。

Result: 发现任务类型交互效应：gist压缩在多跳推理和单跳事实问题上显著优于截断，但时间类问题在压缩下明显变差。机制归因显示：gist抽象提示保留了关系和事件结构但丢弃了日期时间。修改提示后，时间表达保留率从3.05%提升到62.39%（约20倍提升），而命名实体和事件保留率几乎不变（x1.02和x1.11），表明该修复是精确的。在匹配集中，该修改使第2类（时间）问题的judge准确率恢复+0.314（95% CI [0.254, 0.375]）。

Conclusion: gist压缩并非对所有记忆检索类型都有利——它在推理类任务上有效，但会系统地丢失时间信息，损害时间类问题。该问题可通过精确的提示级修复解决，且不影响其他信息类型的保留，证明关键在于抽象过程中的信息选择性保留策略。

Abstract: Gist-based context compression---summarising older conversation history into compact representations---is a common approach in long-horizon language model agents, yet its effect on different types of memory retrieval is poorly understood. We use Salience-Weighted Consolidation (SWC), a biologically-inspired compression framework motivated by sleep-based memory consolidation, as a diagnostic probe to study when gist compression helps and when it hurts. SWC scores conversation history by salience, partitions it into priority tiers, and applies structured gist abstraction to mid-priority content. Evaluating four conditions on all ten LoCoMo conversations---1,935 matched text-only questions in total, 1,501 used in the primary aggregate after excluding Category 5 (adversarial) questions---at temperature 0, we find a consistent task-type interaction: gist compression substantially outperforms truncation on multi-hop reasoning and single-hop factual questions, but temporal questions remain substantially harder under compression, with compressed conditions scoring well below the full-context reference on the conversations where both are evaluated. We trace this failure to a specific mechanism: the gist abstraction prompt preserves relational and event structure while discarding dates and times. A preservation analysis across all ten conversations confirms the mechanism: an approximately 20-fold increase in temporal expression preservation (3.05% to 62.39%) with a one-sentence prompt modification, while named entity and event preservation rates barely change (x1.02 and x1.11), demonstrating that the fix is a precision instrument. The prompt modification recovers +0.314 [0.254, 0.375] judge accuracy on category-2 (temporal) questions in the matched set. Code and results: https://github.com/kyrkewood/sleeping-agent.

</details>


### [243] [Agent Skills Can Be Harmful: An Empirical Study of Skill-Induced Failures in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11888)
*Gen Dong,Yanjie Gao,Liqun Li,Tianyin Xu,Yu Hua,Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种差分分析框架，通过将目标技能引导的任务运行与无技能或语义匹配的参考运行进行对比，系统归因LLM代理中技能引发的功能失败和效率回退。在SkillsBench和SWE-Skills-Bench上识别出307个技能诱导的失败（125个功能失败和182个效率回退），并构建了SkillTriage分类引导归因工具。关键发现包括：技能相关失败很少源于明显不相关的技能，看似相关的技能常导致实现遗漏；效率回退不能仅由提示长度解释；过度验证和重型实现流水线是效率回退最大来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能是扩展LLM代理可复用指导的事实标准机制，但已有工作报告其效果不一致：部分技能提升任务成功率，部分技能无效果、增加token消耗和执行时间，甚至降低成功率。缺乏对技能诱导失败的系统归因分析，使得技能设计和使用缺乏可操作的洞察。

Method: 提出差分分析框架，将目标技能引导的运行与无技能参考运行或语义匹配的技能参考运行进行成对比较，将失败或回退归因到具体技能。在两家基准（SkillsBench和SWE-Skills-Bench）上实例化该框架，构建SkillTriage工具：对成对案例进行规范化、提取差分证据并生成分类归因报告。

Result: 识别出307个技能诱导失败：125个功能失败和182个效率回退。主要发现：（1）功能失败很少由明显不相关的技能引起，看似相关的技能反而使代理错误实现或遗漏任务必需实现元素；（2）效率回退不能仅由提示长度解释；（3）过度验证（67例）和重型实现流水线（30例）是Excessive Procedure类别中最大的来源。

Conclusion: 技能常常将验证清单和构建流程转化为强制性工作，导致功能失败和效率回退。基于这些发现，作者提出面向更安全、更具成本意识的技能复用的研究方向和工具改进建议。

Abstract: Agent skills are the de facto mechanism for extending LLM agents with reusable guidance. A skill can shape the agent's task execution, including planning, tool use, problem-solving, and validation. Prior work reported mixed results of agent skills: some skills improve task success rates, while others have no effect, increase token use and execution time, and even reduce success rates. This paper presents a comprehensive analysis of skill-induced agent failures by attributing task failures and cost regressions to specific loaded skills. We introduce a differential analysis framework that attributes a failure or regression to a skill by comparing a target skill-guided run against a no-skill or semantically matched skill reference run that solves the same task, or solves it more cheaply. We instantiate this framework on SkillsBench and SWE-Skills-Bench, yielding 307 skill-induced failures, including 125 functional failures and 182 efficiency regressions. We also build SkillTriage, a taxonomy-guided attribution tool that normalizes paired cases, extracts differential evidence, and produces triage reports. Our major findings include: (1) Skill induced functional failures are rarely caused by obviously irrelevant skills; instead, seemingly relevant skills often make the agent incorrectly implement or omit task-required implementation elements. (2) Skill-induced efficiency regressions are not explained by prompt length alone. (3) The largest sources within Excessive Procedure are excessive verification and heavy implementation pipelines, contributing 67 and 30 cases, respectively. This shows that skills often turn validation checklists and construction recipes into mandatory work. Based on our findings, we propose research topics and tooling improvements for safer and more cost-aware skill reuse.

</details>


### [244] [Policy-as-logic for robust reasoning over rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11905)
*Rahul Nair,Bastian Lipka,Elizabeth Daly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合符号方法，将政策表示为形式逻辑，在推理时利用语言模型进行事实抽取来接地谓词，并使用答案集求解器进行推理，使回答可解释、可审计且对输入扰动具有鲁棒性，同时相比策略即提示词和策略即代码方法在大多数情况下性能更优，token消耗减少约10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统在回答自然语言查询时（如税务规则、航空公司行李限额）需要遵守书面政策或规则，而现有政策即提示词（policy-as-prompt）和政策即代码（policy-as-code）方法在准确性、鲁棒性和token效率方面存在不足。

Method: 提出混合符号方法：（1）将政策表达为形式逻辑；（2）在推理时利用语言模型进行事实抽取以接地谓词；（3）使用答案集求解器（answer set solver）进行符号推理，将抽取与推理步骤分离。

Result: 该方法在大多数情况下优于政策即提示词和政策即代码方法，实现了约10倍（~10x）的token消耗减少，同时在输入扰动下保持准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，将结构化推理与符号求解器结合生成式模型，对于涉及客观标准的稳健决策具有重要价值。

Abstract: In many practical applications of generative AI systems, from tax rules to airline baggage allowance, responses to natural language queries must respect written policies or rules. We present a hybrid symbolic approach that expresses policies in formal logic and at inference time exploits the representation power of language models for fact extraction to ground predicates, and an answer set solver for reasoning such that responses are interpretable, auditable, and as we show, accurate and robust under input perturbations. Specifically, we show this separation of extraction and reasoning steps outperforms policy-as-prompt and policy-as-code methods in most cases with ~10x reduction in token usage. The results point to the value of structured reasoning and symbolic solvers in conjunction with generative models to make robust decisions involving objective criteria.

</details>


### [245] [OEIS Open: How many conjectures can language models turn into theorems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11941)
*Tom Adamczewski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了OEIS Open基准，包含492个来自OEIS的开放数学猜想（已用Lean形式化验证），并提供了开源的通用语言模型评估代码。配备最小工具集的LM以每次50美元预算解决了其中147个猜想（30%准确率）；在OEIS Open Lite（100个猜想的随机子集）上，以200美元预算评估时最佳LM达到44%准确率。实验表明访问arXiv文献库或使用更复杂的智能体循环并不能提升性能，展示了LM能以适中成本自主解决开放研究猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放数学猜想的自动求解评估多为定制化智能体，缺乏可复现、通用且防作弊（secure against LM cheating）的基准。作者希望构建一个开源基准，让任意通用语言模型都能被公平地评估其在解决真实开放数学猜想上的能力，从而探究LM自主解决研究级数学问题的潜力。

Method: 基于Tsoukalas等人已用Lean形式化的492个OEIS开放猜想构建OEIS Open基准；开发开源评估代码，支持任意通用LM并防止作弊（例如防止LM猜测验证器逻辑或利用评测漏洞）。OEIS Open Lite为100个猜想的随机子集以降低评测成本。在实验中为LM配备最小工具集（如Lean证明搜索等），以不同预算（$50/$200每次尝试）评估，并对比有无arXiv文献访问（476,000篇论文）以及不同复杂度的agent循环。

Result: 配备最小工具集的LM以$50预算解决147/492个猜想（30%得分）；最佳当前LM以$200预算在OEIS Open Lite上达到44%。访问arXiv文献库和更复杂的agent循环均未提升性能。证明LM可以以适中经济成本自主解决开放研究猜想。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使面对数学意义不明确且少有人关注的开放猜想，LM在配备基本工具时也能以较低成本实现可观的成功率（30%-44%）。这为自动数学推理和开放式研究提供了实证支持，同时也指出简单工具集与简洁agent设计可能已足够，而额外的文献检索或复杂循环并非必要。

Abstract: We construct OEIS Open, a benchmark based on 492 open mathematical conjectures from the OEIS, formalized in Lean by Tsoukalas et al. Whereas these conjectures had previously been attempted only with a bespoke agent, our open-source evaluation code runs any generic language model (LM) against them, and is secure against LM cheating attempts. We find that LMs equipped with a minimal set of tools resolve 147 of these conjectures with a budget of \$50 per attempt, scoring 30% on OEIS Open. OEIS Open Lite is a random subset of 100 conjectures for cheaper evaluation. When evaluated with a budget of \$200 per attempt, the best current LM scores 44% on OEIS Open Lite. Giving LMs access to the mathematics literature via 476,000 papers from arXiv did not increase performance on OEIS Open Lite, and nor did using more sophisticated agent loops. The conjectures covered in this work are of uncertain mathematical significance, and most have likely received little previous attention. Nevertheless, our results show that LMs can resolve open research conjectures autonomously and at modest cost.

</details>


### [246] [ExRole: From Team Trajectories to Executable Roles in Multi-Agent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11949)
*Zhou Liu,Chaoyang Han,Zewei Pan,Zeli Su,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ExRole框架，将多智能体系统中的角色从手写提示标签转变为可执行的控制变量，通过学习轨迹中预测未来收益的角色原型，将其解析为可读指令和标记，并通过LoRA秩槽进行路由。在MuSiQue和2WikiMultiHopQA上分别超越单智能体搜索15.0/14.4和13.5/16.1的EM/F1分，相比最强非ExRole基线仍保持11.5/11.6和7.7/9.7的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多智能体系统将角色视为手写的提示标签，与学习到的行为和参数更新脱节。作者认为有用的角色应当是"可执行的控制变量"，即需要能（1）总结预测未来效用的行为，（2）指导后续交互，（3）识别负责该行为的可训练容量。

Method: ExRole采用"轨迹到角色"框架：首先从前缀局部团队轨迹中学习未来感知的角色原型，然后将这些原型解析为可读指令和词元对齐的角色标记，最后可选地通过回合对齐的信用分配路由共享LoRA秩槽。

Result: 在MuSiQue上比单智能体搜索提升15.0/14.4 EM/F1分，在2WikiMultiHopQA上提升13.5/16.1分；对比最强非ExRole控制方法，分别仍保持11.5/11.6和7.7/9.7分的优势。所有对照实验一致表明，轨迹诱导的角色条件优于无角色、手工、随机和打乱的角色设置。Role-Agent-Turn干预实验进一步证明，诱导角色捕获了超越固定智能体身份或回合位置的可迁移行为特化。

Conclusion: ExRole验证了将角色作为可执行控制变量而非静态提示标签的有效性，通过学习到的角色原型能够显著提升多智能体系统的推理性能，且这些角色具备可迁移的行为特化能力，超越了固定的智能体身份或回合位置的限制。

Abstract: Roles provide an interpretable interface for organizing language-model agents, yet most multi-agent systems treat them as hand-written prompt labels disconnected from learned behavior and parameter updates. We argue that a useful role should instead be an executable control variable: it should summarize behavior predictive of future utility, guide subsequent interaction, and identify the trainable capacity responsible for that behavior. We introduce ExRole, a trajectory-to-role framework that learns future-aware role prototypes from prefix-local team traces, resolves them into readable instructions and token-aligned role markers, and optionally routes shared LoRA rank slots with turn-aligned credit. Across MuSiQue and 2WikiMultiHopQA, ExRole improves over single-agent search by 15.0/14.4 and 13.5/16.1 EM/F1 points, respectively. Against the strongest non-ExRole controls, the corresponding gains remain 11.5/11.6 and 7.7/9.7 points. Across both benchmarks, the controlled results consistently favor trajectory-induced role conditioning over role-free, manual, random, and shuffled alternatives. Role-Agent-Turn interventions further show that the induced roles capture transferable behavioral specialization beyond fixed agent identities or turn positions.

</details>


### [247] [Retry, Switch, or Abstain? Learning Strategy-Aware Tool-Use Policies via Controlled Error Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11977)
*Chaoran Chen,Vy Nguyen,Ziji Zhang,Abhinav Gullapalli,Ziyi Wang,Yuxuan Lu,Dakuo Wang,Jing Huang,Zhou Yu,Jin Lai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BENCH2ROBUST框架，将无故障的工具使用基准转化为可控随机、可解性受控的随机环境，研究LLM代理在工具故障下的恢复能力。通过贝叶斯工具记忆（BTM）提供运行时恢复上下文，结合课程控制的强化学习，在7个模型、4个模型族和两个多轮基准上验证了鲁棒性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理通常在工具调用可靠成功的环境中训练和评估，但实际部署中工具可能短暂、持续或静默地失败。稳健恢复需要的不仅仅是简单重试——代理可能需要重试相同路径、切换到替代方案，或识别无可行路径并停止。这类鲁棒性问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 提出BENCH2ROBUST框架，将无故障基准转化为可控随机环境，支持场景可控的可解性，使每个回合明确需要重试、切换或停止。研究两种互补干预：贝叶斯工具记忆（BTM）提供结构化运行时恢复上下文，以及课程控制的强化学习训练恢复行为。

Result: 在7个模型、4个模型族和两组多轮基准上，工具故障产生了近乎普遍的鲁棒性差距。在保留的Retail任务上，BTM在不重新训练的情况下将鲁棒性提升最多16.8个百分点；强化学习学习到互补的恢复行为，在无推理时BTM时依然有效。两者结合在注入条件下达到40.8-45.5%的鲁棒性，同时保持无故障性能。

Conclusion: 稳健的工具使用受益于将环境特定的恢复知识与学习到的恢复行为相结合，提示未来系统应同时关注离线知识注入和在线学习两条互补路径来提升代理在真实部署中的工具故障恢复能力。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents are commonly trained and evaluated in environments where tool calls succeed reliably, yet deployed tools can fail transiently, persistently, or silently. Robust recovery therefore requires more than repeated retries: an agent may need to retry the same path, switch to an alternative, or recognize that no viable path remains. We present BENCH2ROBUST, a framework that converts failure-free tool-use benchmarks into controlled stochastic environments with scenario-controlled solvability, where episodes explicitly require retrying, switching, or stopping after available paths are exhausted. We use BENCH2ROBUST to study two complementary interventions: structured runtime recovery context through Bayesian Tool Memory (BTM), and curriculum-controlled reinforcement learning. Across 7 models from 4 families and two multi-turn benchmark families, tool failures produce a near-universal robustness gap. On held-out Retail tasks, BTM improves robustness by up to 16.8 percentage points without retraining, while RL learns complementary recovery behavior that remains beneficial without inference-time BTM. Combining the two reaches 40.8-45.5% under injection while preserving failure-free performance. These results suggest that robust tool use benefits from combining environment-specific recovery knowledge with learned recovery behavior.

</details>


### [248] [Claim-Level Reliability Assessment for Efficient Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11994)
*Sen Xu,Wei Wang,Shixi Liu,Jixin Min,Yingwei Dai,Zhibin Yin,Yirong Chen,Junlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以"声明级证伪"为原则的测试时扩展方法（CLR），将推理迹压缩为关键声明集合，通过语义证伪与非线性可靠性评分来重新分配测试时计算，在匹配预算下普遍优于pass@1和self-consistency。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体推理迹的评估常因常规token的信号稀释而掩盖关键错误；同时，在固定模型能力下生成完全正确的解难度极大，而识别错误声明只需找到单个决定性缺陷，这一不对称性未被充分利用。

Method: CLR采用无训练框架，将推理迹压缩为决策关键声明集合以隔离逻辑锚点；将重点从构建完整解转向语义声明证伪，利用"构造难、证伪易"的不对称性；通过非线性可靠性评分系统性压缩高置信错误轨迹的生存空间，替代额外采样来重新分配测试时计算。

Result: 在四个LLM和四个推理基准上、匹配预算条件下，CLR普遍优于pass@1与self-consistency。在GPT-OSS-20B/CMIMC25上，CLR较pass@1提升27.15个百分点，将self-consistency准确率从77.50%提升至82.19%，同时节省37.0%的token。

Conclusion: 声明级证伪作为测试时扩展原则是有效的，通过将计算从额外采样转向针对性验证，能在不增加计算预算甚至减少token消耗的情况下显著提升推理性能。

Abstract: We propose claim-level falsification as a principle for test-time scaling and instantiate it through Claim-Level Reliability Assessment (CLR), a training-free framework that reallocates test-time compute from additional solution sampling to targeted verification. Since whole-trace evaluation often obscures decisive errors due to signal dilution from routine tokens, CLR condenses each reasoning trace into a compact set of decision-critical claims, thereby isolating its logical anchors. Furthermore, recognizing the inherent difficulty of generating entirely correct solutions under fixed model capabilities, CLR shifts the focus to semantic falsification. This approach exploits a fundamental asymmetry between solution construction and claim refutation. Constructing a valid solution requires a flawless reasoning path, whereas refuting an incorrect claim requires identifying only a single decisive flaw. This targeted search for negative evidence systematically compresses the survival space of high-confidence incorrect traces, effectively suppressing erroneous consensus via nonlinear reliability scoring. Across four LLMs and four reasoning benchmarks under matched budgets, CLR generally improves upon pass@1 and self-consistency. On GPT-OSS-20B/CMIMC25, for instance, CLR exceeds pass@1 by 27.15 percentage-points and raises self-consistency accuracy from 77.50\% to 82.19\% with 37.0\% fewer tokens.

</details>


### [249] [CTBench: Evaluating Troubleshooting Capabilities of AI Agents in Realistic Telecom Network Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12002)
*Xingyu Yan,Tingting Dai,Antonio De Domenico,Mohamed Sana,Nicola Piovesan,Changchang Li,Bowen Liu,Kun Jiang,Mengjie Zhang,Dingcheng Shan,Jing-Cheng Pang,Chenwei Wu,Sijie Wu,Lianying Chao,Haoran Cai,Jiantao Ye,Xubin Li,Simon Mark Lucas,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CTBench，一个用于评估智能体是否具备电信运维工程师能力的公开基准，聚焦根因分析与路径恢复任务，实验发现现有SOTA智能体在路径恢复任务中表现良好但在根因分析中表现欠佳，且即使给出看似正确的最终答案也常常缺乏证据支撑的诊断过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测未能准确模拟真实网络特征，也没有在部分可观测的电信环境（涉及多厂商、设备、协议和接口）下评估智能体，缺乏一个能衡量智能体是否像称职电信排障工程师一样工作的基准。

Method: 构建CTBench公开基准，聚焦根因分析与路径恢复两类任务。每个任务由领域专家构造并标注丰富的任务元数据（包括黄金证据步骤）。采用专家贴地的评估指标，同时衡量最终答案和诊断证据质量。通过代表性harness-model组合进行实验评测。

Result: SOTA智能体在路径恢复任务中定位端点表现优秀，但在根因分析中普遍表现不足，尤其在接口状态、链路层、服务管理及其他运维故障上挣扎。更重要的是，即使智能体给出看似合理或正确的最终答案，也常无法提供运维实践所需的证据贴地诊断。此外，路径恢复任务通常更消耗资源，但更大资源消耗并不一定带来更好的诊断效果。

Conclusion: CTBench揭示了现有智能体在电信运维场景下的关键短板：诊断能力与实际运维要求之间存在显著差距，尤其体现在证据链的构建上。这为未来智能体在部分可观测电信环境下的研究与评估提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly considered for automating network operations and maintenance, where engineers must diagnose network faults, optimize configurations to enhance services, and reduce operational costs while acting under strict constraints. However, existing evaluations fail to accurately model real network characteristics or assess agents under partially observable telecom environments with diverse vendors, devices, protocols, and interfaces. In this paper, we introduce CTBench, a public benchmark for assessing whether an agent behaves like a competent telecom troubleshooting engineer. CTBench focuses on root cause analysis and path restoration. Each task is constructed by experts and annotated with rich task metadata, including golden evidence steps. CTBench uses expert-grounded metrics that evaluate both final answers and the diagnostic evidence. Experiments with representative harness-model combinations show that state-of-the-art agents perform very well at identifying endpoints in path-restoration tasks but, more generally, underperform in root cause analysis. In particular, agents struggle with interface state, link-layer, service-management, and other operational faults. Most importantly, even when agents produce plausible or correct final answers, they often fail to provide the evidence-grounded diagnoses required in operational practice. Our results further show that path restoration is generally more resource expensive, yet larger resource usage does not necessarily translate into better diagnosis.

</details>


### [250] [Mechanist: AI as a Scientific Instrument for Discovering the Mechanisms of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12036)
*Mengru Wang,Junfeng Fang,Shuofei Qiao,Zhenqian Xu,Haoming Xu,Haoxiong Wang,Shumin Deng,Linyi Yang,Zhixiang Cui,Xin Xu,Yunzhi Yao,Buqiang Xu,Fei Shen,Haozhe Luo,Yunxiang Wei,Ningyu Zhang,Julian McAuley,Tat Seng Chua,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mechanist，一个利用AI作为科学仪器来自主发现AI智能背后机制的智能体系统。该系统整合了约13,000篇可解释性论文的知识图谱和涵盖26个领域的4,300万篇论文的多学科数据库，并配备32种基础方法库。Mechanist在机制假设生成和实验执行上优于Claude Code及现有AI科学家系统，并展示了从发现模型行为到解释和控制AI模型的完整进展链，包括发现跨模态不安全特征迁移的安全风险、构建信念的机制理论，以及将机制洞见转化为改善模型性能和引导DNA序列生成的实践干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在各个领域取得了显著成功，但其能力背后的机制和潜在风险仍不为人知。随着AI开发速度加快且日益自动化，机制探索仍主要依赖人工，导致模型能力与我们对其理解和控制之间的差距不断扩大。作者旨在通过自主系统弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建了Mechanist智能体系统，包含三部分：(1)约13,000篇论文的可解释性知识图谱；(2)涵盖26个领域的4,300万篇论文多学科数据库；(3)32种用于机制分析、因果干预和验证的基础方法库。系统利用AI作为科学仪器执行自主机制发现流程。

Result: 与Claude Code和现有AI科学家系统相比，Mechanist能生成更有价值的机制假设并更可靠地执行实验。Mechanist展示了三个层次的进展：(1)发现科学实验室中的跨模态不安全特征迁移的安全风险；(2)发展出关于信念形成的机制理论，揭示模型如何表征世界知识、形成信念、推断他人信念及这些机制在预训练中的涌现；(3)将机制洞见转化为实践干预，改善模型性能并引导科学基础模型生成具有指定特性的DNA序列。

Conclusion: Mechanist证明了AI驱动的自主机制发现是可行的，能够从发现模型行为，到解释AI模型，再到控制AI模型进行实际干预。这为弥合AI能力与可理解性、可控性之间的鸿沟提供了新的范式。

Abstract: AI models have achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, yet the mechanisms underlying their capabilities and the risks they may pose remain poorly understood. As AI development becomes faster and increasingly automated, mechanistic exploration remains largely manual, widening the gap between what models can do and our ability to understand and control them. To bridge this gap, we introduce Mechanist, an agentic system that uses AI as a scientific instrument for the autonomous discovery of mechanisms underlying AI intelligence. To support autonomous mechanistic discovery, we construct an interpretability-focused knowledge graph of approximately 13,000 papers and integrate it with a multidisciplinary database of 43 million papers spanning 26 fields. We further curate a library of 32 foundational methods for mechanism analysis, causal intervention, and validation. Compared with Claude Code and existing AI-scientist systems, Mechanist generates more valuable mechanism hypotheses and executes experiments more reliably. Mechanist also demonstrates a progression from discovering model behaviors to explaining and controlling AI models. Specifically, Mechanist first uncovers a counterintuitive safety risk in scientific laboratories, showing that unsafe traits can transfer across modalities through apparently safe training data. Mechanist then develops a mechanism theory of belief, revealing how models represent world knowledge, form beliefs, infer the beliefs of others, and how these mechanisms emerge during pretraining. Finally, Mechanist translates these mechanistic insights into practical interventions that improve model performance across diverse scenarios and steer scientific foundation models toward generating DNA sequences with specified properties.

</details>


### [251] [Graph-Structured Rubrics: Compiling Rubrics into Typed Evaluation Graphs for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12097)
*Xi Chen,Jie Mu,Mo Xuan,Qun Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出图结构化评分标准（Graph-Structured Rubrics, GSR），将评分标准编译成语义独立的类型化评估图，通过命名端口组合标准节点与变换/归约/门控算子，并以任务特定的Readout将图汇点映射为分数或偏好。在GPT-OSS-120B下，GSR在四个点式数据集上比Prometheus式评分在精确分数一致性上提升0.62-6.75个百分点，并在两个偏好基准上取得数值上最高的端到端成对准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的评估器通常将标准仅视为提示上下文或扁平条件，虽指定了评判内容，但标准间如何组合（criterion composition）是隐式的，即便自然语言规则已给出组合方式。这种隐式组合导致评估结果不稳定、不可控，缺乏对评分逻辑的显式建模。

Method: GSR将评分标准编译为与响应无关的类型化评估图：标准节点负责获取评判结果，变换（transformation）、归约（reduction）和门控（gating）算子通过命名端口组合这些评判；任务特定的输出映射（Readout）将唯一的汇点转换为分数或偏好。编译过程会拒绝格式错误或类型不兼容的图。点式评估先分别评判各标准维度再进行图聚合；成对评估复用同一张图，每个标准下对每个候选各做一次评判。

Result: 在GPT-OSS-120B下，GSR在四个点式数据集上比Prometheus式评分在精确分数一致性上提升0.62-6.75个百分点；在两个偏好基准上，在原生平局与弃权策略下取得数值上最高的端到端成对准确率。

Conclusion: 显式地将评分标准编译为结构化评估图，能够更准确地遵循评分逻辑，提升点式评分一致性与成对偏好评估的端到端准确率，是一种优于将标准仅作扁平提示的处理方式。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluators commonly treat rubrics as prompt context or flat criteria: they specify what to judge but leave criterion composition implicit, even when natural-language rules state it. We introduce Graph-Structured Rubrics (GSR), which compiles a rubric into a response-independent typed evaluation graph before observing responses. Criterion nodes elicit judgments; transformation, reduction, and gating operators compose them through named ports; and a task-specific output mapping, termed Readout, converts the unique sink into a score or preference. Compilation rejects malformed or type-incompatible graphs. Pointwise evaluation judges rubric dimensions separately before graph aggregation; pairwise evaluation reuses the graph with one judgment for each candidate under every criterion. Under GPT-OSS-120B, GSR improves exact score agreement by 0.62--6.75 percentage points over Prometheus-style scoring on four pointwise datasets and achieves the numerically highest end-to-end pairwise accuracy on two preference benchmarks under native tie and abstention policies.

</details>


### [252] [GUIDE: Governed Unified Intelligence for Document-to-Artifact Generation in Enterprise Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12133)
*Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Mohammadreza Sediqin,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GUIDE，一个受治理的多智能体框架，用于处理企业指南文档的异构多模态内容（文本、表格、图像）。该框架通过共享版本化规则库、模式验证的智能体间契约和端到端溯源追踪，整合六个专业智能体（解析、VLM提取、一致性检查、评估、HITL升级、个性化工件生成），在120份真实企业文档上实现96%成功率、提取3,896条规则（71.4%自动批准）、生成812个部署就绪工件，并将每份文档处理时间从2-3天缩短至40-125分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业指南文档具有异构性和多模态性（文本、复杂表格、嵌入式图像），现有LLM和VLM系统存在幻觉内容、表格结构退化以及缺乏超越提取的受治理工作流（包括验证和工件生成）等问题，导致企业需人工处理，每份文档耗时2-3天。

Method: GUIDE采用受治理的多智能体架构：构建在共享版本化规则库之上，使用模式验证的智能体间契约和端到端溯源追踪。框架包含六个专业智能体：解析智能体、VLM驱动的提取智能体、一致性检查智能体、评估智能体、人机协同（HITL）升级智能体，以及面向角色定制的工件合成智能体。

Result: 在120份真实企业指南文档上评估，GUIDE实现96%的文档成功率，提取3,896条规则且71.4%自动批准，生成812个部署就绪工件，将每份文档的处理时间从2-3天缩短至40-125分钟。

Conclusion: GUIDE通过受治理的多智能体框架有效解决了企业指南文档处理的难题，在准确性、自动化率和效率上均取得显著提升，大幅缩短了文档处理时间，同时通过HITL和溯源机制确保了治理合规性。

Abstract: Enterprise guideline documents are heterogeneous and multimodal, combining narrative text, complex tables, and embedded images. Existing LLM and VLM systems face hallucinated content, table structure degradation, and lack governed workflows extending beyond extraction to validation and artifact generation. This leaves enterprises to perform this manually, consuming 2-3 days per document. To address this, we introduce GUIDE, a governed multi-agent framework built on a shared versioned rule store with schema-validated inter-agent contracts and end-to-end provenance tracking. Six specialized agents handle parsing, VLM-driven extraction, consistency checking, evaluation, human-in-the-loop (HITL) escalation, and persona-tailored artifact synthesis. Evaluated on 120 real-world enterprise guideline documents, GUIDE achieves 96% document success, extracts 3,896 rules with 71.4% auto-approved, produces 812 deployment-ready artifacts, and reduces turnaround to 40-125 minutes per document.

</details>


### [253] [Who Thinks Best Depends on How Long You Let Them: Budget-Dependent Rankings in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12150)
*Rodrigo Guedes de Souza,Alison R. Panisson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"大语言模型在不同推理条件下排名稳定"的假设，通过将token生成预算在7个等级（64-4,096）间变化，在3个推理基准（共56,476次推理）上评估4个模型。研究发现：(i) 3-19%的题目呈现非单调行为（预算增加反而准确率下降），且该现象具有模型特异性；(ii) 模型排名在所有基准上随预算变化而反转（McNemar检验p<0.01）；(iii) 预言机分析显示模型间存在高达+27.8个百分点的互补性，在预算受限时最为显著；(iv) 预算感知路由器可捕获跨域预言机差距的14.1%，预算特征在域内有帮助（+1.6至+5.7pp）但具有域特异性且损害迁移（-1.2pp）。这些结果支持采用预算条件化的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的大模型评估假设模型排名在推理条件下保持稳定，但该假设缺乏实证检验。作者旨在挑战这一假设，系统性地探究token生成预算（即模型可生成的最大token数）如何影响模型性能与排名。

Method: 作者设置了7个不同级别的token生成预算（从64到4,096），在3个推理基准上对4个模型进行了共56,476次推理评估。采用McNemar检验验证排名反转的统计显著性；通过预言机分析（oracle analysis）测度模型间的互补性；并设计了预算感知路由器（budget-aware router）来探索预算特征对模型选择和路由的效用。

Result: (i) 3-19%的题目在控制截断后仍呈现非单调行为（准确率随预算增加而下降），跨模型重叠率仅为6-14%；(ii) 所有基准上模型排名随预算变化显著反转（p<0.01）；(iii) 预言机揭示模型互补性达+27.8pp，在受限预算下最突出；(iv) 预算感知路由器跨域捕获14.1%的预言机差距，域内加预算特征提升1.6-5.7pp但损害跨域迁移（-1.2pp）。

Conclusion: 模型排名对token生成预算高度敏感，当前固定预算的评估协议可能产生误导性结论。作者主张采用预算条件化的评估协议（budget-conditioned evaluation），以更全面地反映模型在不同推理资源约束下的实际能力。

Abstract: Standard evaluation of large language models assumes stable model rankings across inference conditions. We challenge this assumption by varying the token generation budget, i.e., the maximum tokens a model may produce, across seven levels (64--4,096), evaluating four models on three reasoning benchmarks (56,476 inferences). We report four findings: (i) 3--19% of items exhibit non-monotone behavior (accuracy decreasing with more budget), even after controlling for truncation, and this phenomenon is model-specific (cross-model overlap: 6--14%). (ii) Model rankings reverse across budgets on all benchmarks ($p {<} 0.01$, McNemar). (iii) Oracle analysis reveals model complementarity up to $+27.8$pp, most pronounced at constrained budgets. (iv) A budget-aware router captures 14.1% of the oracle gap cross-domain; budget features help within-domain ($+1.6$ to $+5.7$pp) but are domain-specific and hurt transfer ($-1.2$pp). These results argue for budget-conditioned evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [254] [How to Spend Your Oracle Budget: Practical Guidance for Protein Structure Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12192)
*Aleksandra Kalisz,Jack Simons,Krisztina Sinkovics,Noam Ghenassia,Shikha Surana,Henry Moss,Paul Duckworth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了在预算受限的oracle条件下，用于蛋白质结构预测模型引导的多种方法（FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N和O3），发现没有单一方法在所有预算和oracle设置下都占优：O3在低预算下最有效，而FK-steering和DPO在预算增加时表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质结构预测的基础模型在某些目标上仍不可靠，外部oracle可以标记并纠正这些失败，但生物oracle成本高昂，oracle预算成为关键约束。现有多种引导方法在预算消耗方式上各异，但缺乏系统性的比较来指导方法选择。

Method: 将O3方法扩展应用到蛋白质结构预测模型，并在两个蛋白质目标（钙调蛋白1CLL和大肠杆菌天冬氨酸转氨甲酰酶9EEH）上系统比较O3、FK-steering、DPO和Best K-of-N四种方法在不同oracle预算条件下的性能。

Result: 评估结果表明没有单一方法在所有预算和oracle下一致占优。O3在低oracle预算下最有效，而FK-steering和DPO在预算增加时性能提升明显。这是首个针对oracle预算感知引导的实际参考基准。

Conclusion: 研究成果提炼为对实践者可操作的建议，帮助在真实世界oracle预算约束下选择合适的方法。

Abstract: Foundation models for protein structure prediction remain unreliable on certain targets. External oracles can flag and correct these failures, but biological oracles are expensive, making oracle budget a critical constraint. Existing guidance methods, such as FK-steering, DPO, and Best K-of-N sampling, differ in how they spend this budget, yet no systematic comparison exists to guide method selection. To bridge this gap, we benchmark these methods alongside the recently proposed Optimisation Over Outputs (O3), which applies off-the-shelf optimisers within a generative model's latent subspace. We extend the usage of O3 to protein structure prediction models. Overall, our work provides the first practical reference for oracle budget-aware guidance. Our evaluation on two protein targets, calmodulin (1CLL) and E. coli aspartate transcarbamoylase (9EEH), reveals that no single method consistently dominates across all budgets and oracles. Specifically, O3 proves most effective at low oracle budgets, while FK-steering and DPO demonstrate improved performance as the budget increases. We distil these findings into actionable recommendations for practitioners operating under real-world oracle-budget constraints.

</details>


### [255] [An Agentic Workflow for Legacy HPC Modernization: Converting the Two-Electron-Integral Core of GAMESS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12249)
*Yuzhong Shen,Masha Sosonkina,Peng Xu,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多智能体的工作流，用于在工业规模上现代化改造遗留Fortran代码。三个提示词专门化的智能体角色在版本控制的规范（由智能体自身编写和修订）下协作，人类仅保留少量审批关口。该工作流在GAMESS量子化学软件包（48年历史）的两电子积分例程转换中验证：12个源文件、56,448行代码、225个子程序从固定格式Fortran 77转换为自由格式Fortran 2008，全部通过51项测试验证（612次测试运行中化学相关差异为零）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留Fortran代码现代化改造的核心问题是规模：单个转换是常规操作，但代码库极其庞大，且计算科学界大部分此类工作长期未完成。作者希望评估智能体驱动的自动化工作流能将这种委托推进到何种程度，即在保持安全性的前提下实现大规模生产级代码转换。

Method: 提出由三个提示词专门化的智能体角色组成的代理工作流（使用Claude Code，横跨四代模型），在智能体自身编写和修订的版本控制规范下运行，人类仅控制少量审批关口。安全性由领域内继承的精确验证预言机保障——GAMESS标准测试套件输出的能量被视为规范，采用逐位精确复现作为合并准则（第12位小数的偏差即视为失败）。智能体在隔离的worktree中并行工作。

Result: 在GAMESS两电子积分例程上完成实际验证：12个源文件、56,448行代码、225个子程序全部从Fortran 77转换为Fortran 2008。所有文件通过51项测试验证（49项GAMESS标准测试加2项额外计算），612次测试运行中化学相关差异为零，全部文件也通过Jenkins持续集成测试。安全委托的边界恰好落在验证预言机可观测的范围处。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了一个关键观点：安全委托的边界恰好位于验证预言机停止观测之处。当领域内存在精确的验证标准（如逐位能量复现）时，多智能体工作流可以在生产规模上可靠地执行遗留代码现代化改造，且人类干预需求极小。这套方法论为计算科学中大规模遗留代码转换提供了可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Modernizing legacy Fortran is a problem of volume: the transformations are individually routine, but the codebases can be enormous, and across much of computational science the work simply goes undone. We propose an agentic workflow that takes this work on at production scale, and we set out to measure how far such delegation can reach. In this work, three prompt-specialized agent roles operate under a version-controlled specification that the agents themselves authored and revised, while humans hold a small number of gates. The arrangement is kept safe by an exact verification oracle inherited from the domain, and the boundary of safe delegation lies exactly where that oracle stops seeing.
  We apply the proposed workflow in a case study, converting the two-electron-integral routines of GAMESS (General Atomic and Molecular Electronic Structure System), a mature quantum-chemistry package with a 48-year development history, from fixed-form Fortran 77 to free-form Fortran 2008. The scope of this work was twelve source files, 56,448 lines, and 225 subroutines for computing electron repulsion integrals. The agents ran as three Claude Code roles in isolated worktrees, and the work spanned four Claude model generations. Because the GAMESS group ships a standard test suite whose printed energies its user community treats as canonical, we could adopt bit-for-bit reproduction of those energies as the merge criterion, where a deviation in the twelfth decimal place counts as a failure rather than drift. All twelve source files pass a 51-test validation battery comprising the 49 standard GAMESS tests and two additional calculations, and across 612 test runs the number of chemistry-relevant differences is zero, and every file also passes the Jenkins tests that are used for continuous integration.

</details>


### [256] [VAKRA: Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Across APIs and Retrieval Under Tool-Use Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12282)
*Ankita Rajaram Naik,Anupama Murthi,Benjamin Elder,Siyu Huo,Raavi Gupta,Abhinav Jain,Praveen Venkateswaran,Abdulhamid Adebayo,Danish Contractor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VAKRA基准，用于评估企业场景中智能体在结构化API和文档检索上的推理能力。基准包含8,000多个可执行API、62个领域，覆盖三种难度递增的任务设置。评测发现最好的模型在单跳任务上仅达70.4%，组合型API上降至50-51%，且随推理深度增加性能下降超50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将API使用和文档知识检索能力分开评测，但企业场景中部署的智能体必须同时跨越结构化API和文档集合进行推理。作者指出这一评估空白，因此需要构建一个能同时评测这两种能力的综合基准。

Method: 构建VAKRA基准，包含62个领域中超过8,000个可执行API，设计三种难度递增的任务设置：多样化API交互风格、结构化API上的多跳推理、带自然语言工具使用策略约束的多源推理。正确性通过重新执行预测的工具调用与实时API对比验证，允许多种有效路径。使用固定的ReAct框架隔离模型能力与智能体架构的影响，评测前沿和开源权重模型。

Result: 最佳模型在单跳端点式任务上仅达到70.4%的准确率，在组合型API上降至50-51%；推理深度增加时性能下降超过50%；策略约束问题暴露严重失败，未回答查询上最低仅有2.4%的准确率。痕迹分析显示失败集中在语言中介推理环节——实体消歧、跨源知识对齐——而非工具调用的机制层面。

Conclusion: 即使最先进的模型在企业级API和知识检索组合任务上仍表现不足，主要瓶颈在于语言层面的推理能力（如实体消歧和跨源对齐），而非工具调用的执行机制。这为未来智能体推理能力提升指明了方向。

Abstract: Agents deployed in enterprise settings must reason across structured APIs and document collections, yet existing benchmarks evaluate these capabilities in isolation. We introduce VAKRA (e\textbf{V}aluating \textbf{A}PI and \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{R}etrieval \textbf{A}gents), a benchmark of over $8{,}000$ executable APIs across $62$ domains with tasks spanning three settings of increasing difficulty: diverse API interaction styles, multi-hop reasoning over structured APIs, and multi-source reasoning with natural-language tool-use policy constraints. Correctness is verified by re-executing predicted tool calls against live APIs, accommodating multiple valid paths. Using a fixed ReAct harness to isolate model capabilities from agent architecture, we evaluate frontier and open-weight models and find that even the best model achieves only 70.4\% on single-hop endpoint-style tasks and drops to 50--51\% on compositional APIs; performance degrades by over 50\% as reasoning depth increases, and policy-constrained questions expose severe failures (as low as 2.4\% on unanswerable queries). Trace analysis shows failures concentrate at language-mediated reasoning - entity disambiguation, cross-source grounding, rather than tool invocation mechanics. Code is available https://github.com/IBM/VAKRA. Dataset is available https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-research/VAKRA

</details>


### [257] [Constructing Dynamic Master Logic Models as Knowledge Graphs for Complex System Diagnostics Using Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12304)
*Saman Marandi,Yu-Shu Hu,Mohammad Modarres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动化框架，利用检索增强生成（RAG）和大语言模型（LLM），将系统描述自动构建为动态主逻辑（DML）模型，并以知识图谱（KG-DML）形式表示。该框架在小型系统的先前工作基础上，扩展至更大规模的复杂系统，并通过对退役沸水反应堆低压冷却剂注入系统的应用，验证了其在诊断推理、安全评估和失效传播分析中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的DML模型构建依赖专家对技术文档的解读，这种方法难以扩展到复杂系统。随着系统规模增大，人工构建DML模型的成本和难度急剧上升，因此需要一种自动化方法将技术文档转化为可执行的DML模型，以支持诊断推理和可靠性分析。

Method: 该框架利用检索增强生成（RAG）和大语言模型（LLM），沿DML层级结构进行定向检索，自动从系统描述中提取功能目标与结构元素之间的关联，构建知识图谱表示的DML模型（KG-DML）。模型构建过程中保留功能依赖关系和显式逻辑关系，并通过多层级验证方法（包括层特定精确率/召回率、逻辑门一致性及整体结构完整性）进行评估。

Result: 将框架应用于退役沸水反应堆的低压冷却剂注入（LPCI）系统，多次运行结果一致，表明模型重建具有稳定性。验证结果表明，自动构建的KG-DML能够有效支持诊断推理、安全评估、向上失效传播和向下依赖追踪等功能。

Conclusion: 自动化KG-DML构建能够将技术文档转化为可执行的、可用于诊断和可靠性分析的功能模型，克服了传统专家驱动的DML构建方法在可扩展性上的局限，为复杂系统建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Dynamic Master Logic (DML) provides a hierarchical framework for representing system behavior by linking functional objectives to underlying structural elements. However, DML construction typically relies on expert interpretation of technical documentation, limiting scalability for complex systems. This study presents a framework for automated construction of DML models from system descriptions and their representation as Knowledge Graphs (KG-DML), using Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Large Language Models as enabling tools. Building on prior work with small-scale systems, the framework extends automated KG-DML construction and evaluation to substantially larger and more complex systems. Model construction proceeds across the DML hierarchy using targeted retrieval while preserving functional dependencies and explicit logical relationships. The resulting KG-DML supports diagnostic reasoning, safety assessment, upward failure propagation, and downward dependency tracing. A multi-level validation methodology evaluates layer-specific precision and recall, logical gate consistency, and overall structural integrity. Application to the Low-Pressure Coolant Injection system of a decommissioned Boiling Water Reactor demonstrates consistent reconstruction across repeated runs. The results show that automated KG-DML construction can transform technical documentation into executable functional models for diagnostic and reliability analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [FarSky: Task-Aware Latent-Space Coupling for Generative Intra-Hour Solar Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11254)
*Yann Fabel,Bijan Nouri,Milon Miah,Niklas Blum,Luis F. Zarzalejo,Julia Kowalski,Robert Pitz-Paal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FarSky是一个生成式太阳辐照度预测框架，通过多任务自编码器学习天空图像的共享潜在表征，再利用潜在扩散模型生成未来的潜在状态并解码为辐照度预测。在西班牙Almería的多年ASI数据集上，FarSky在确定性、概率预测以及爬坡事件检测方面均优于现有方法，预测技能提升高达11个百分点，F1分数超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的太阳辐照度预测对光伏电力并网至关重要。现有深度学习方法虽已提升预测精度，但受限于确定性预测且难以预测辐照度爬坡事件，缺乏对预测不确定性的建模能力。

Method: FarSky采用两阶段框架：(1) 多任务自编码器学习图像重建和辐照度估计的共享潜在表征，实现任务感知的潜在空间耦合；(2) 基于近期观测的潜在扩散模型生成未来潜在状态，直接解码为辐照度预测。通过随机采样天然获得概率预测。

Result: 在两个独立测试数据集上，FarSky在确定性和概率预测方面均取得最佳性能，预测技能较现有方法提升最多11个百分点；爬坡事件检测F1分数超过60%，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将生成模型与任务感知的潜在空间耦合相结合，是提升太阳能预测性能的有效途径，尤其能显著改善爬坡事件的预测能力。

Abstract: Accurate solar irradiance forecasting is essential for the reliable integration of photovoltaic power into modern electricity grids. All-sky imagers (ASI) provide high-resolution observations of clouds, making them well suited for intra-hour forecasting. Recent deep learning approaches have substantially improved forecast accuracy but are often limited by deterministic predictions and a reduced capability to anticipate ramp events. This work proposes FarSky, a generative forecasting framework that leverages latent-space coupling to learn task-aware representations of sky images. A multi-task autoencoder first learns a shared latent representation for image reconstruction and irradiance estimation. A latent diffusion model then generates future latent states conditioned on recent observations, from which irradiance forecasts are directly decoded. Probabilistic forecasts are inherently obtained through stochastic sampling. The framework is developed using a multi-year ASI dataset acquired at the Plataforma Solar de Almería, Spain, and evaluated on two independent test datasets against persistence, state-of-the-art end-to-end, and generative forecasting approaches. FarSky achieves the best overall deterministic and probabilistic forecasting performance, improving forecast skill by up to 11 percentage points. Furthermore, it substantially improves ramp event detection over existing methods, achieving F1-scores above 60%. These results demonstrate the potential of combining generative models with task-aware latent-space coupling for solar forecasting.

</details>


### [259] [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256)
*Jonathan A. Karr,Grigorii Khvatskii,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 商业AI检测器无法区分轻度AI编辑与完整LLM生成，导致合规的AI辅助编辑被误判为学术不端的风险高于使用humanizer规避检测的行为。实验显示，轻度"仅润色摘要"编辑被标记率为64-80%，而经Undetectable AI人工作处理后逃逸率极高（<4%被标记，FNR>96%）。因此检测器分数不应作为学术不端的独立证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构普遍使用商业AI检测器维护学术诚信，但检测器在政策层面存在根本性缺陷：无法区分合法的轻量AI编辑与完整LLM草稿，可能将两者都视为不端行为。作者旨在量化这一政策失灵，揭示其对遵守AI使用指南的诚实学者造成的系统性不公正。

Method: 对已发表英文摘要（四个学科领域；对比2013-2015与2023-2025两个时段）进行受控实验。在tau=0.50下以人类/AI代理标签区分；使用Pangram和GPTZero检测器对"仅润色摘要"的轻量编辑及未修改原文进行检测；同时测试Undetectable AI人工化处理后的逃逸率；并通过统计检验分析学科差异及长token密度、学术词汇表密度与检测分数的关联。

Result: 轻度"仅润色摘要"编辑被Pangram/GPTZero标记率为64-80%；未修改的2023-2025原文标记率为9-15%，非STEM学科显著高于STEM（p<0.001）；高检测分数与长token密度和学术词汇表密度相关而非仅由作者意图决定；经Undetectable AI人工化后逃逸近乎彻底，被标记率<4%，漏检率>96%。

Conclusion: 诚实的AI辅助编辑面临的制裁风险反而高于借助humanizer进行规避的行为，这构成了严重的政策悖论。检测器分数不能作为学术不端的独立证据，需要重新设计检测与执法策略。

Abstract: Institutions use commercial AI detectors for academic integrity, yet detectors cannot distinguish AI editing from full LLM drafts and may treat both as misconduct. In a controlled study of published English abstracts (four domains; 2013 to 2015 vs. 2023 to 2025), we quantify this policy failure under proxy human/AI labels at tau=0.50. Light "refine abstract only" edits, a proxy for guideline-compliant AI assistance, are flagged at 64 to 80% (Pangram/GPTZero). Unmodified 2023 to 2025 originals are flagged at 9 to 15%, with non-STEM rates far above STEM (p<0.001); elevated scores track long-token and Academic Word List density, not authorship intent alone. After Undetectable AI humanization, evasion is near-total: fewer than 4% of AI-labeled rewrites remain flagged (post-humanization detection rate <4%; FNR >96%). Honest AI-editing results in a higher sanction risk than humanizer-assisted evasion. Therefore, detector scores should not serve as standalone misconduct evidence.

</details>


### [260] [Basin: Efficient and Extensible Numerical Optimization in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11279)
*Johan Larsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍Basin，一个面向Rust编程语言的数值优化库，为用户提供统一、一致的问题定义与求解接口，涵盖多种求解器并原生支持约束优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值优化是科学领域的基础任务，广泛应用于模型拟合、仿真校准、机器学习训练和工程参数选择等场景。需要一个一致且全面的数值优化库来简化问题的定义与求解。

Method: Basin为用户提供单一、一致的API来表述和求解优化问题，内置丰富的求解器目录，并提供一流的约束支持。

Result: Basin作为一个Rust数值优化库，具备统一的优化问题表述方式、广泛的求解器集合以及完善的约束处理能力。

Conclusion: Basin通过提供统一的数值优化接口和全面的求解器支持，降低用户在Rust中解决优化问题的门槛。

Abstract: Basin is a numerical optimization library for the Rust programming language. Numerical optimization is the task of finding the inputs that minimize a function, and it is a fundamental element across the sciences: fitting a model to data, calibrating a simulation, training a machine learning model, or choosing engineering parameters that minimize cost. Basin gives users a single, consistent way to both state and solve such problems, with a broad catalog of solvers and first-class support for constraints.

</details>


### [261] [Federated Learning for Distributed CNC Tool Wear Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11281)
*Afsana Khan,Morris Stallmann,Marcin Pietrasik,Charis Kouzinopoulos,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习在CNC刀具磨损预测中的应用，通过模拟客户端分布刀具轨迹数据，证明联邦学习性能接近集中式学习且显著优于本地客户端模型，支持分布式制造环境下的协同刀具磨损预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNC加工中刀具磨损预测对产品质量和工艺可靠性至关重要，但工业环境中数据分散在多个机器/站点/组织之间，且存在数据共享限制，传统机器学习方法难以直接应用，需要一种无需传输原始数据即可协同训练模型的框架。

Method: 采用联邦学习框架，将刀具轨迹数据分布到模拟客户端以构建设置联邦学习场景，并将联邦模型与集中式参考模型及本地客户端基线模型进行对比评估。

Result: 联邦学习在刀具磨损预测上取得了与集中式学习相当的性能，同时显著优于仅在各自本地数据上训练的客户端模型。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够有效支持分布式CNC制造环境下的协同刀具磨损预测，在保障数据隐私的同时实现接近集中式训练的预测精度。

Abstract: Tool wear prediction is an important task in CNC machining, where accurate monitoring of tool condition supports product quality and process reliability. Machine learning methods have shown potential for this task, but their use in industrial environments is limited by the distributed nature of machining data and by restrictions on data sharing between machines, sites, or organizations. Federated learning offers a suitable framework for this setting by enabling collaborative model training without transferring raw operational data. This paper investigates federated learning for CNC tool wear prediction. Tool trajectories are distributed across simulated clients to represent a federated learning scenario. The federated models are compared against centralized references and local client baselines. Results show that federated learning achieves performance close to centralized learning and improves significantly over local client models. These findings indicate that federated learning can support collaborative tool wear prediction in distributed CNC manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [262] [Terminal Symmetry as a Decision Resource: Statewise Refinement for Anytime Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11318)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"决策-资源"视角下的终端对称性（terminal symmetry）框架，通过"传输-精炼-验证"（transport-refine-certify）的分解结构，将终端（完成状态）的对称性转化为可复用的决策资源。所提出的方法（\method{}）实例化该原理，在CAD装配、小程序构建和精确填充等任务上，通过状态级刷新显著提升了anytime AUC，并在GRN OOD场景的1,135个目标移除片段中取得了最低的平均封顶验证成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多顺序构建任务在完成时表现出精确对称性，但执行过程却是有方向性的且依赖历史。作者希望利用这种"完成时的对称性"这一结构特性来指导决策，将其从被忽视的静态属性转化为主动的决策资源。

Method: 提出决策-资源视角下的终端对称性，包含三个核心组件：（1）传输（transport）：过程证据提供方向性，终端对应关系将结构传输到等价结果；（2）精炼（refine）：实现状态证据在转换后被接受后精炼其当前决策相关性；（3）验证（certify）：固定验证器认证执行。\method{}实例化该原理：使用片段固定的传输过程结构、状态受限的过程秩、转换后刷新的状态相关残差秩，以及序数秩的meet操作，其top-k集合恰好是两个提议前缀的并集。

Result: 在CAD装配、Mini-Programs和精确填充任务中，状态级刷新分别将anytime AUC提升了最多6.77、21.75和8.68个百分点。在GRN OOD场景的1,135个目标移除片段中，\method{}在三个尺度下均取得了最低的平均封顶验证器成本（相比GRN和CDGS风格的规划器）。状态级信号还在不同聚合和调度器组织中有效迁移。

Conclusion: 终端对称性可以作为一种可复用的决策资源，用于指导有方向的构建过程。所提出的transport-refine-certify框架及其实例化方法，在多个领域任务上验证了该思路的有效性，并暗示了后转换状态下动态与静态方法之间存在严格分离。

Abstract: Many sequential construction tasks exhibit exact symmetry at completion while their execution remains directed and history-dependent. We develop a decision-resource view of terminal symmetry: process evidence supplies directionality, terminal correspondence transports that structure across equivalent outcomes, realized-state evidence refines its current decision relevance after transitions, and a fixed verifier certifies execution. This decomposition yields transport--refine--certify. \method{} instantiates the principle with an episode-fixed transported process structure, its state-restricted process rank, a state-dependent residual rank refreshed after accepted transitions, and an ordinal rank meet whose top-$k$ set is exactly the union of the two proposal prefixes. The meet provides a completion guarantee under prefix coverage and attains the tight worst-case verifier-query bound under the corresponding prefix information model; a two-state construction predicts a strict post-transition dynamic--static separation. Across CAD assembly, Mini-Programs, and exact-fill packing, statewise refresh improves anytime AUC by up to $6.77$, $21.75$, and $8.68$ points, respectively. On 1,135 target-removal episodes from the official GRN OOD scenes, \method{} attains the lowest mean capped verifier cost at all three scales among the compared GRN and CDGS-style planners. The statewise signal also transfers across aggregation and scheduler organizations. Terminal symmetry thereby becomes a reusable decision resource for directed construction.

</details>


### [263] [Contextual Quality-Diversity Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning for HVAC Control in Tropical Commercial Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11324)
*Tran Le Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CQD-ERL，一种用于热带水冷制冷站及其风侧超视控制的上下文质量-多样性进化强化学习控制器。该控制器维护一个按数据驱动运行环境、每日天气与负荷模式聚类及环境不变行为描述子联合索引的产品归档，由无梯度进化操作符与共享重放缓冲区的soft-actor-critic策略梯度操作符共同填充，每次执行动作前均通过确定性安全护盾过滤。控制器在代表新加坡商业建筑潜在负荷、冷却塔趋近温度及湿度约束的两层降阶环境中训练，并在全年回测中与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习控制器往往收敛到单一标量化策略，难以应对暖通空调系统多目标、多工况的复杂控制需求，尤其是在热带气候下制冷站与风侧联合控制场景中缺乏鲁棒性与可解释性。

Method: 采用CQD-ERL架构：构建由环境上下文（每日气候与负荷聚类）和行为描述子联合索引的"产品归档"，结合无梯度进化算子与SAC策略梯度算子（共享重放缓冲区）共同更新策略集合，并在动作执行前加入确定性安全护盾以保障运行安全。训练环境为两层降阶模型，涵盖负荷、冷却塔趋近温度和湿度约束。

Result: 控制器在全年回测中与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行对比评估，验证了所提方法在热带水冷制冷站超视控制中的有效性。

Conclusion: CQD-ERL通过维护多样性策略归档替代单一策略，结合安全护盾与进化-策略梯度的混合优化机制，为暖通空调系统提供了更具适应性和安全性的强化学习控制方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a contextual quality-diversity evolutionary reinforcement-learning controller, CQD-ERL, for the supervisory control of a tropical, water-cooled chiller plant and its associated air side. Rather than converging to a single scalarised policy, the controller maintains a product archive of specialised policies indexed jointly by a data- driven operating context, a cluster of daily weather and load regime, and a context-invariant behaviour descriptor, filled by a gradient-free evolutionary operator and a soft-actor-critic policy-gradient operator that share one replay buffer. Every action is filtered through a deterministic safety shield before execution. The controller is trained on a two-tier reduced-order environment representing the latent load, cooling-tower approach and humidity constraints of a Singapore commercial building, and is evaluated over a full annual backtest against an ASHRAE Guideline 36 baseline.

</details>


### [264] [Long-Horizon Forecasting of Complete Financial Statements with Forma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11327)
*Travis L. Johnson,Jiannan Jiang,Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Yihao Chen,Lauren Falvey,Donal O'Cofaigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProForma-20Q基准和Forma模型，用于联合预测完整财务报表未来1-20个季度的78个报表行项目，Forma通过将报表表示为（账户、季度、数值）元组并使用掩码元组高斯似然进行训练，在预测精度上超越所有基线（包括经典机器学习、梯度提升、时序基础模型及前沿大语言模型），且预测区间覆盖良好、基本满足会计恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作无法在超过一年的时间跨度上联合预测完整财务报表，而在折现现金流估值中大部分企业价值恰好位于该窗口之外，因此缺乏一个可复现的基准来评估长期财务报表预测方法。

Method: 构建ProForma-20Q基准，包含78个报表行项目、1-20个季度前瞻预测，基于匿名公司历史报表和行业代码，使用变化空间R²评分。Forma是一个transformer架构，将报表视为（账户、季度、数值）元组集合，通过最大化掩码元组高斯似然进行训练，支持无需重训的情景分析。

Result: Forma在所有竞争者中表现最佳，包括经典机器学习、链式梯度提升、零样本时序基础模型和前沿大语言模型；其优势随着预测跨度增大而扩大；高斯预测区间从不欠覆盖；预测几乎满足会计恒等式，且精确一致性可在无显著统计精度损失下恢复；钉住未来收入路径可锐化财务报表其余部分的预测。

Conclusion: 专业化的专家训练优于通用化的大规模模型，元组接口设计兼具灵活性与准确性，为长期财务报表预测及估值场景提供了可复现、高质量的基准和模型。

Abstract: Specialist training beats generalist scale when forecasting financial statements. To our knowledge, no prior work jointly forecasts complete financial statements beyond one year, yet in a discounted-cash-flow valuation most firm value sits past that window. We release ProForma-20Q, a reproducible benchmark for forecasting 78 statement line items 1-20 quarters ahead, for anonymized firms, from past statements and an industry code, scored by change-space $R^2$. On it, Forma, a transformer that reads statements as sets of (account, quarter, value) tuples and maximizes a masked-tuple Gaussian likelihood, beats every competitor we field: classical machine learning, chained gradient boosting, a zero-shot time-series foundation model, and frontier large language models. Its lead widens with horizon, where valuation needs accuracy most, and its Gaussian predictive intervals never under-cover. Forma's forecasts nearly satisfy accounting identities; exact coherence is recoverable at no statistically significant accuracy cost. Its tuple interface supports scenario analysis without retraining, and we show that pinning future revenue paths sharpens the rest of the statement.

</details>


### [265] [Weightless Fine-Tuning: Personalizing LLMs via Logit-Space Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11342)
*Bohan Zhang,Anqi Ni,Yixin Wang,Paramveer S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT)，一种无需训练、在解码时运行的微调方法，通过跨前缀传输算子将作者训练序列的监督残差迁移到当前提示中，在logit空间实现对SFT分布效果的近似，无需更新模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的监督微调（SFT）在个性化等场景中存在成本过高的问题，因为每位作者都需要独立的权重访问、优化、存储和重训练。需要一种更轻量的替代方案来近似SFT的效果。

Method: WFT在解码时运行，无需权重更新。它先在作者训练序列上计算监督残差（supervised residuals），然后通过从dropout诱导的跨协方差中估计的跨前缀传输算子（cross-prefix transport operator），将这些残差传递到当前提示中。该算子捕捉一个上下文中的扰动如何在另一个上下文的预测中传播，从而用logit空间修正替代基于梯度的参数更新。

Result: 在三个LaMP个性化基准上，WFT取得了跨数据集的平均最佳性能，在个别任务上达到或超越SFT，并平均优于其他轻量级基线。在预算受控的比较中，WFT以不到7%的有效计算量接近SFT性能。Logit级别分析显示，WFT与SFT引发的logit偏移在95%的next-token概率质量上余弦相似度达0.875。

Conclusion: WFT能够在不修改模型权重的情况下，有效捕捉监督适应的分布性效果，为个性化等场景提供了计算高效的SFT替代方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a standard approach for adapting LLMs to a target distribution, but in settings such as personalization, where each author requires separate weight access, optimization, storage, and retraining, its costs become prohibitive. We propose Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT), a training-free decoding-time method that approximates the distributional effect of SFT without weight updates. WFT computes supervised residuals on an author's training sequence and transports them to the current prompt through a cross-prefix transport operator estimated from dropout-induced cross-covariance. The operator captures how a perturbation at one context propagates to predictions at another, replacing gradient-based parameter updates with logit-space corrections. On three LaMP personalization benchmarks, WFT achieves the best average performance across datasets, matches or exceeds SFT on individual tasks, and outperforms other lightweight baselines on average. In a budget-controlled comparison, WFT approaches SFT performance using less than 7% of the effective computation. Logit-level analysis shows a cosine similarity of 0.875 between the logit shifts induced by WFT and SFT over 95% of the next-token probability mass, suggesting that WFT captures the distributional effect of supervised adaptation without modifying model weights.

</details>


### [266] [Dynamics Models for Offline Hyperparameter Selection in Real-World RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11349)
*Jordan Coblin,Han Wang,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将校准模型（calibration models）应用于真实工业场景——市政水处理厂，以支持强化学习超参数离线选择，克服在线实验成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在真实系统部署中面临超参数选择困难，尤其当模拟器不可用且在线实验成本高昂时。先前基于离线数据的校准模型仅在该简单仿真环境中验证过，缺乏在真实工业场景中的实证评估。
